### [1] [On the binding and stability of CeO2 molecules on low-index Au surfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33338)
*Ying-Cheng Chen,Ahmad Ahmad,Anter El-Azab*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用自旋极化密度泛函理论（DFT），系统研究了CeO2分子在Au(001)、Au(110)、Au(111)三种表面上的结合与稳定性，揭示了分子构型、结合能、电荷转移及电子结构随结合位点变化的规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多通过自组装生长的多相薄膜涉及金属相与氧化物相的共沉积，此时氧化物可能以化学计量分子形式存在于金属表面。量化这类分子氧化态在金属表面的稳定性、结合与扩散行为，对于理解自组装至关重要。

Method: 采用自旋极化密度泛函理论计算，系统考察CeO2分子的构型、结合能、结合自由能、结构稳定性、有效电荷及投影电子态密度（PDOS）在Au(001)、Au(110)、Au(111)表面不同结合位点上的变化。

Result: 分子态CeO2在所有Au表面上均比其原子组分解离态更有利；弯曲构型比线性构型更稳定，其中Ce原子位于中空位（hollow site）、O原子位于顶位（top site）。结合能约为或弱于-1 eV的构型在室温以上存在概率较低。电荷转移使CeO2分子在所有表面均发生极化，但极化仅在Au(001)与Au(110)表面的强结合态中更为显著。

Conclusion: 识别出CeO2分子与Au表面结合的三种情形：情形1为对称键的化学吸附、情形2为不对称键的化学吸附、情形3为对称键的物理吸附。Au(001)与Au(110)表面同时存在三种结合情形，而Au(111)仅出现情形2和情形3。

Abstract: Many multi-phase thin films grown by self-assembly involve co-deposition of both metallic and oxide phases that grow into the same film. Under such conditions, the oxide species may be present in stoichiometric molecular form on the surface of the metallic phase. Quantifying the stability, binding, and diffusion of such molecular oxide states on metallic surfaces is critical for understanding self-assembly. In this work, we investigate the binding and stability of CeO2 molecules on Au surfaces using spin-polarized density functional theory calculations. Specifically, we examine how the configuration of the CeO2 molecule, its binding energy and binding free energy, structural stability, effective charge, and projected electronic density of states vary among various binding sites on Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) surfaces. Molecular CeO2 was found to be more favorable on all Au surfaces than its atomic constituents. It was also found that the bent CeO2 molecule configuration is more stable than the linear counterpart, with the Ce atom on the hollow site and the O atoms on the top sites. In addition, the configurations of the CeO2 molecule on Au surfaces with a binding energy around or weaker than -1 eV were found to have a low probability of existence above room temperature. Furthermore, the charge transfer was found to occur in such a way that the CeO2 molecule becomes polarized on all surfaces, but the polarization is stronger only for the strongly bonded states on Au(001) and Au(110) surfaces. Finally, three different cases of how the CeO2 molecule binds to Au(001), Au(110), and Au(111) were identified: case 1: chemisorption with symmetric bonds, case 2: chemisorption with asymmetric bonds, and case 3: physisorption with symmetric bonds. These three cases of CeO2 binding are present on Au(001) and Au(110), but only cases 2 and 3 occur on Au(111).

</details>


### [2] [Magnetically Guided Sequential Multi-Filler Alignment Enables Anisotropic Heat Pathways in Stretchable Composites for Soft Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33381)
*Altynay Kaidarova,Viktor Naenen,Isidro Florenciano,Ruben Windey,Xiaoyan Qing,Laurens Snels,Juhyung Park,Tom Van der Donck,Jeroen Soete,David Seveno,Francisco Molina-Lopez*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"先取向后共混"的两步策略，通过磁场将镍微粒在空气中排列成竖直导热骨架，再用液态金属/镍混合物填充颗粒间隙，制得兼具高纵向导热（7.5 W/(m·K)）、极低横向导电（<10⁻¹² S/m）与超软机械顺应性（约160 kPa）的复合材料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可拉伸、贴合皮肤的电子器件需要同时具备高纵向导热、机械柔软性和横向电绝缘的热界面材料，而在颗粒填充复合材料中这些要求通常相互矛盾：提高颗粒含量以缩短颗粒间距会不可避免地带来机械刚化和面内导电，现有颗粒取向工艺也难以有效解耦多功能性。

Method: 采用分层两步工艺：第一步"先取向后共混"，在空气中通过磁场将镍（Ni）微粒取向排列成高有序、密集堆积的竖直导热骨架（无粘性弹性体基体干扰，便于处理大量颗粒）；第二步将液态金属（LM）/镍混合物渗入取向骨架，桥接颗粒间缝隙以降低颗粒间热阻。

Result: 复合材料实现了7.5 W/(m·K)的纵向导热率，横向电导率低于10⁻¹² S/m，机械柔软顺应性约160 kPa，并成功应用于热电发电机的耦合层和可穿戴设备的手指贴附散热垫。

Conclusion: 该分层两步策略有效解耦了导热、电绝缘与机械柔软性之间的矛盾，为可拉伸皮肤贴合电子器件的热界面材料设计提供了新路径。

Abstract: Stretchable, skin-conformal electronics demand thermal interface materials that combine high through-plane heat transport with mechanical softness and lateral electrical insulation. In particle-filled composites, these requirements are typically antagonistic. Heat transport is governed not only by the intrinsic thermal conductivity of the fillers but, more critically, by thermal resistance at the interparticle interface. As the interparticle distance is reduced by increasing the particle content, mechanical reinforcement and in-plane electrical conductivity become unavoidable. The existing particle alignment processing strategies result in inefficient uncoupling of multifunctionality. Here, we present a hierarchical two-step process strategy to induce strong conductivity anisotropy in a soft composite. First, an align-before-blend process forms a scaffold of magnetically aligned nickel (Ni) microparticles in air before polymer infiltration. The absence of the viscous elastomer matrix during the particle alignment facilitates handling large amounts of particles, which assemble into highly ordered and densely packed vertical heat-conduction pathways. Second, a liquid metal (LM)/Ni mixture is infiltrated into the aligned scaffold to bridge interparticle gaps, minimizing interparticle thermal resistance. The composite reaches 7.5 W/(m K) through-plane thermal conductivity with lateral electrical conductivity below $10^{-12}$ S/m and ultrasoft mechanical compliance (approximately 160 kPa), enabling coupling layers in thermoelectric generators and finger-mounted heat-spreading pads for wearable technologies.

</details>


### [3] [Unveiling ferroelectricity in wafer-scale beta-phase In$_2$Se$_3$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33405)
*Kyungmin Yang,Hyunmin Cho,Jihwan Jeon,Tae Hyeon Park,Seojeong Ro,Hye Jung Kim,Jung-Hoon Lee,Jaewon Wang,Hyeon Suk Shin,Young-Min Kim,Chang Woo Myung,Roy H. Olsson,Seunguk Song,Deep Jariwala*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文发现β-In₂Se₃（一种通常被认为中心对称的范德华半导体）中存在由五元层内皮米级畸变产生的双稳态极性序，该极性态可被垂直电场切换，并稳定到双层极限（约1.9 nm），兼具铁电电容与半导体晶体管沟道双重功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将铁电性推进到极限厚度是存储器硬件的核心挑战，因为传统三维铁电体在减薄时会因去极化和界面效应而退化，需要寻找能在原子级厚度下保持稳定可切换极性的材料。

Method: 采用金属有机化学气相沉积（MOCVD）在2英寸晶圆上生长β-In₂Se₃薄膜，通过垂直电场切换其极性，并制备电容器阵列与铁电半导体晶体管进行表征，测试开关电压一致性、耐疲劳性及保持特性。

Result: 该极性态具有面外和面内分量，可被垂直电场切换，并持续到双层极限（约1.9 nm）；估算矫顽场为0.9 MV/cm，表观剩余极化强度为11.0 μC/cm²。100个器件间开关电压离散度低于5%，耐久性达10⁷次循环，预计十年保持特性良好。

Conclusion: β-In₂Se₃凭借超薄尺度下稳定的极性序及其与半导体能隙的共存，使同一薄膜既能作为电容器的开关层，又能作为铁电半导体晶体管的沟道，为极限厚度铁电存储与集成器件提供了可行平台。

Abstract: Pushing ferroelectricity to the ultimate thickness limit is a central challenge for memory hardware, because conventional three-dimensional ferroelectrics degrade through depolarization and interfacial effects when thinned. Here we identify $β$-In$_2$Se$_3$, a van der Waals semiconductor regarded as centrosymmetric, as a host of bistable polar order arising from a picometre-scale distortion within each quintuple layer. The polar state has out-of-plane and in-plane components, is switched by a vertical electric field, and persists to the bilayer limit (~1.9 nm) with an estimated coercive field of 0.9 MV/cm and an apparent remanent polarization of 11.0 $μ$C/cm$^2$. Films grown by metal-organic chemical vapor deposition on 2-inch wafers yield capacitor arrays with a switching-voltage spread below 5% across 100 devices, an endurance of $10^7$ cycles and a projected ten-year retention. Because the polar state coexists with a semiconducting gap, one film serves as both the switching layer of a capacitor and the channel of a ferroelectric semiconductor transistor.

</details>


### [4] [Electron--phonon interactions in a pseudo--atomic orbital basis: superconductivity, transport, spectroscopy and thermal emission from one interpolated vertex](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33473)
*Marco Buongiorno Nardelli*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于伪原子轨道（PAO）固定基组、无需人工调参的电子-声子耦合计算方法，在开源代码 PAOFLOW 中实现，并在铅和硅上验证了与传统 Wannier 方法的等效精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子-声子相互作用决定超导、电阻率、光吸收和激发寿命等性质，但第一性原理计算传统需用最大局域化 Wannier 函数将耦合插值到致密布里渊区网格，这类方法依赖材料特异性的人工调参，阻碍了高通量自动化计算。

Method: 采用由赝势自动生成的伪原子轨道（PAO）固定基组实现电子-声子顶点的插值，集成于开源代码 PAOFLOW；输入可来自密度泛函微扰理论或位移超胞计算，并纳入长程场与自旋轨道耦合，免去手动调参。

Result: 在相同输入下与成熟代码得到的电子-声子顶点一致；在铅上复现了声子色散、超导能隙、临界温度、电阻率、光电子谱扭结及回旋质量，在硅上捕获了声子辅助光吸收、晶圆发射率和载流子迁移率。

Conclusion: 该无参数框架可实现高通量、自动化的电子-声子计算，适用于大规模材料数据库，有望替代依赖人工调参的传统 Wannier 方案。

Abstract: Electron--phonon interactions govern superconductivity, electrical resistivity, optical absorption and electronic excitation lifetimes in solids. First--principles calculations require interpolating this coupling onto dense Brillouin--zone grids, traditionally using maximally localized Wannier functions that demand material--specific tuning. Here we show that a fixed basis of pseudo--atomic orbitals--automatically generated from pseudopotentials and implemented in the open--source code PAOFLOW captures the electron--phonon vertex with equivalent accuracy and no manual tuning. Computed from density--functional perturbation theory or displaced supercells, and incorporating long--range fields and spin--orbit coupling, the vertex matches established codes on identical inputs. We validate the method on lead, reproducing its phonon dispersion, superconducting gap, critical temperature, resistivity, photoemission kink and cyclotron masses, and on silicon, capturing phonon--assisted optical absorption, wafer emissivity and carrier mobilities. This parameter--free framework enables high--throughput, automated electron--phonon calculations across large materials databases.

</details>


### [5] [Low-barrier-height Hf$_\text{1-x}$Zr$_\text{x}$O$_2$ ferroelectric tunnel junctions with large ON-state currents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33495)
*Shaoqing Ding,Jinyuan Yao,Eknath Sarkar,Emmanuel Quezada,Thomas N. Jackson,Susan Trolier-McKinstry,Suman Datta,Ying Liu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文研究了以超薄HZO（≤5nm）为势垒的铁电隧道结在低至2K温度下的电输运行为，发现低温下直接量子隧穿主导并伴生陷阱辅助隧穿，高温下转为热激活导电；通过最小模型揭示势垒高度显著低于能带对齐预测值，归因于浅陷阱带的出现，为高性能FTJ存储器开辟了新路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铁电隧道结（FTJ）在非易失性存储、神经形态计算等领域前景广阔，HZO因其CMOS兼容性成为势垒层首选材料，但要实现FTJ的技术承诺，必须深入理解通过铁电势垒的载流子输运机制。

Method: 制备以超薄HZO（≤5nm）为势垒的FTJ，在低至2K的温度范围内测量其电学输运特性；分析不同温度区间的导电机制（直接量子隧穿、陷阱辅助隧穿与热激活传导），并构建一个能解释全部实验现象的最小模型。

Result: 低温下直接量子隧穿占主导，并伴有陷阱辅助隧穿贡献；随温度升高，热激活导电逐渐取代隧穿。模型计算显示势垒高度显著低于能带对齐图像所预测的数值，这一意外发现归因于浅陷阱带的出现。

Conclusion: 浅陷阱带降低了有效势垒高度，揭示了同时实现大ON态电流、高隧穿电阻比（TER）和改善温度稳定性的新途径，为FTJ的高速存储应用提供了此前未被探索的方向。

Abstract: Ferroelectric tunnel junctions (FTJs) hold great promise for nonvolatile memory, neuromorphic computing, and other applications. Hf$_\text{1-x}$Zr$_\text{x}$O$_2$ (HZO) has become a leading candidate for the tunnel barrier due primarily to its compatibility with CMOS technology. Recent advances on HZO based FTJs have highlighted the importance of understanding the electrical transport through a ferroelectric tunnel barrier for fulfilling the technological promises of FTJs. In this paper we report investigation of HZO FTJs featuring a barrier of ultrathin HZO ($\leq$5nm) measured down to a temperature of 2 K. Direct quantum tunneling was found to dominate at low temperatures, along with contributions from trap-assisted tunneling. As the temperature increases, conduction by thermal activation takes over. We developed a minimal model that accounts for all experimental observations, revealing a barrier height significantly lower than that of the band alignment picture, an unexpected finding attributable to the emergence of a shallow trap band. This finding reveals a previously unexplored pathway toward simultaneously achieving large ON-state currents, high tunnel electroresistance and improved temperature stability for the high-speed memory applications of FTJs.

</details>


### [6] [ILMlip: fast and universal machine-learning interatomic potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33538)
*Jonas Hänseroth,Christian Dreßler*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: ILMlip 是一系列为吞吐速度和零样本精度同时优化的等变守恒消息传递型机器学习原子间势，在单个 GPU 上分子动力学速度可达 MACE（medium）的约 1000 倍、NEP89/DPA-4.1C 的 5 倍，同时大规模模型零样本 Matbench Discovery F1 达到 0.91，逼近当前最强模型（0.935），并支持微调以达到参考级精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大规模预训练原子间势越来越精确但越来越昂贵，训练与推理开销巨大，难以用于高通量、大规模或长时间分子动力学模拟。因此需要一种在保持高零样本精度的同时、显著提升推理速度与结构效率的等变保守势。

Method: ILMlip 采用等变、保守的消息传递势，对每个组件围绕吞吐量进行排列设计，遵循"每对原子计算量最小、每原子按需计算"的原则。提供 6 种规模（参数量 1.4×10^5 到 2.8×10^7），在材料、分子和固体酸数据上预训练；通过 ASE 在单个 GPU 上运行分子动力学，并与 MACE、NEP89、DPA-4.1C 等基准对比；在六种材料上对 S 尺寸模型进行微调，验证接近从头算力的误差。

Result: 零样本 Matbench Discovery 上 ILMlip 六种尺寸 F1 分数为 0.75 至 0.91，最大模型与当前领先者（F1=0.935）仅差 0.03。速度上，ASE 单 GPU 分子动力学比 MACE（medium）快约 10^3 倍，比 NEP89 和 DPA-4.1C 快最多 5 倍。微调后 S 尺寸模型接近微调 MACE 的从头算力误差，在 Cu fcc 上每原子每步约 50 ns 即达到参考级精度。

Conclusion: ILMlip 在零样本精度与推理速度之间取得了优异平衡，适用于高通量、大尺度或长时模拟场景，并因其可迁移性成为微调的强起点；各组件可独立复用，为速度/精度比更优的架构提供基础。实现已开源。

Abstract: Large-scale pretrained machine-learned interatomic potentials keep growing more accurate as new models appear at a rapid pace, and more expensive: the current leader of Matbench Discovery as of November 2026 (F1 score 0.935) has $9.9\times10^7$ parameters and took 3.6 thousand GPU-hours to train. The ILMlip potentials presented here are optimised for speed as well as zero-shot accuracy. They are equivariant, conservative message-passing potentials in which every component is arranged for throughput: little work per pair of atoms and as much as needed per atom. Through ASE on one GPU, they run molecular dynamics up to about $10^3$ times faster than state-of-the-art message-passing potentials such as MACE (medium) and up to five times faster than NEP89 and DPA-4.1C. At the same time, six sizes with $1.4\times10^5$ to $2.8\times10^7$ parameters, pretrained on materials, molecules and solid acids, reach F1 scores of 0.75 to 0.91 on Matbench Discovery, placing the largest within 0.03 of the current leader. They therefore suit high-throughput, large or long simulations, and, being transferable, they are strong starting points for fine-tuning. Fine-tuned to six materials, the model of size S comes close to the ab initio force errors of a fine-tuned MACE, so it reaches reference-level accuracy at about 50 ns per atom and step (Cu fcc). Their components can be adopted separately, as a starting point for architectures with even better ratios of speed to accuracy. The ILMlip implementation is available at https://github.com/jhaens/ilmlip

</details>


### [7] [Effects of Yttrium doping on Proton Conductivity in Ba(Fe, Co, Zr, Y)O$_{3-δ}$ Cathode Materials for Proton Ceramic Fuel Cells](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33543)
*Chiyoung Kim,Ryan Jacobs,Jun Meng,Jack H. Duffy,Kyle S. Brinkman,Yueh-Lin Lee,Harry W. Abernathy,Dane Morgan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用从头算分子动力学（AIMD）模拟与热力学缺陷模型，解耦了Y掺杂对BCFZY钙钛矿阴极中质子扩散率与质子浓度的影响，研究了三种组成（BCFZ、BCFZY0.1、BCFY）的质子输运机制，并揭示气体条件对Y掺杂效果及质子输运机理转变的重要作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质子陶瓷燃料电池（PCFC）在中温下能高效转换能量，但阴极氧还原动力学缓慢限制了其性能。三重导电钙钛矿BCFZY作为空气电极虽有前景，但Y掺杂对质子导电率的具体作用机理尚不清晰，尤其是质子扩散率与质子浓度两种效应的贡献未能解耦，且实验报道的增强效果与气体条件的关系有待阐明。

Method: 采用从头算分子动力学（AIMD）模拟，评估三种组成（BaCo0.4Fe0.4Zr0.2O3-δ即BCFZ、BaCo0.4Fe0.4Zr0.1Y0.1O3-δ即BCFZY0.1、BaCo0.4Fe0.4Y0.2O3-δ即BCFY）的质子示踪扩散率；同时建立涵盖水合反应、氧化反应和氢化物生成反应的热力学缺陷模型，估算温度依赖的质子浓度；并将扩散率与浓度效应解耦后评估质子导电率。

Result: 从BCFZ到BCFY，Y含量增加使500 °C下的质子扩散率提高1.7%。在PCFC运行气氛条件下，质子浓度和质子导电率分别预计提高3.8%和5.3%。这些增幅小于还原条件下实验报道的较大增强，表明气体条件对评估Y掺杂效应至关重要。在低氧分压条件下，主导的质子输运机制从羟基（OH_O•）转变为氢化物（H_O•）物种。

Conclusion: 本研究为BCFZY阴极中的质子输运提供了机理层面的认识，并指出Y掺杂的增益在PCFC实际运行气氛下较为温和，同时揭示了低氧分压下质子输运由羟基向氢化物机制转变。这些发现为优化下一代PCFC中质子导电空气电极提供了设计原则。

Abstract: Protonic ceramic fuel cells (PCFCs) offer efficient energy conversion at intermediate temperatures, but their performance is often constrained by sluggish oxygen reduction kinetics at the cathode. Abstract: Foundation machine-learning interatomic potentials transfer well across chemical and structural space, yet their reliability for highly disordered metallic states is less established. Metallic glasses are a demanding benchmark: they have traditionally been modeled with Embedded Atom Method (EAM) potentials fitted to specific compositions, while ab initio molecular dynamics remains too expensive at the required sizes and timescales. Using the prototypical Cu-Zr-Al glass, we show that foundation potentials capture structural disorder, reproducing the structure, quenching dynamics, and elastic, mechanical and thermal properties of the amorphous state at near first-principles accuracy. We benchmark MACE foundation models against a classical EAM potential along molecular dynamics quenches, validating against density functional theory and experiment, first at Cu46Zr46Al8 and then across the good glass-forming region of the ternary. We further test two specialization routes: fine-tuning on glass structures generated with Lennard-Jones surrogate models, and on automatically generated small periodic cells (ASSYST). Models pre-trained on databases that include non-equilibrium liquid and amorphous configurations, such as OMAT, are accurate and transferable across composition out of the box, clearly outperforming the composition-specific EAM potential. Fine-tuning on target glass structures brings only modest gains, while specialization on small crystalline cells degrades the description of disorder. The readiness of foundation potentials for disordered metals is therefore set mainly by the diversity of their pre-training data rather than by system-specific fine-tuning, shifting the modeling effort from bespoke dataset generation to pre-training coverage and careful validation of the resulting dynamics.

Method: 采用混合分子动力学-蒙特卡洛（MD-MC）模拟，系统考察溶液中玻璃体网络随温度的重构与解离，并通过构建温度-组成相图定量分析溶解临界温度与交联浓度的关系。

Abstract: Vitrimers are dynamic polymer networks characterized by associative bond exchange, which enables topological rearrangement while preserving the overall network connectivity. However, how this dynamic connectivity evolves in the presence of a solvent, particularly as a function of temperature, remains poorly understood. Here, we employ hybrid molecular dynamics Monte Carlo simulations to elucidate the temperature-dependent restructuring and dissociation of vitrimer networks in solution. By constructing temperature composition phase diagrams, we demonstrate that the critical temperature for network dissolution increases nonlinearly with crosslink concentration. We find that the network disintegration begins well below the critical dissolution temperature, indicating that the loss of network connectivity can occur even before the onset of macroscopic dissolution. However, the network undergoes extensive restructuring around the critical temperature for a highly crosslinked system, resulting in the formation of numerous smaller, disconnected networks. Concurrently, the number of intrachain dynamic bonds increases with temperature. Interestingly, complete disintegration into individual polymer chains is not observed; instead, the solution comprises a broad distribution of smaller network fragments. These findings reveal the complex interplay between solvent-induced network disintegration and dynamic bond redistribution, providing molecular level insights into the dissolution of vitrimer networks and their potential for solvent assisted recycling.

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Modeling disordered systems from first principles across the full composition range is a long-standing computational challenge. We present a code package implementing the extended generalized quasichemical approximation (EGQCA), which describes solid solutions through the statistics of a finite set of clusters and enables their thermodynamic modeling at arbitrary compositions and temperatures. The package automates the complete workflow and interfaces with both DFT codes and universal machine-learned interatomic potentials (uMLIPs). We show that replacing DFT with uMLIPs reduces the cost of the cluster calculations by up to five orders of magnitude while preserving the thermodynamic predictions. To make computationally demanding properties tractable, we additionally introduce a cluster-selection algorithm that identifies the most relevant configurations. Together, these developments establish EGQCA as a fast and accurate framework for the thermodynamic modeling of solid solutions and a practical route toward high-throughput screening of disordered materials.

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于广义Koopmans条件的轨道密度依赖约束方法，通过约束Kohn-Sham有效势为轨道密度泛函，消除电子自相互作用误差，使LDA/GGA的带隙平均绝对误差改善约1 eV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kohn-Sham密度泛函理论的自相互作用误差导致轨道能量对其自身分数占据呈非物理的非线性依赖，从而低估带隙并引入系统性误差。作者旨在消除自相互作用，使原子轨道本征能量独立于其分数占据（即满足广义Koopmans条件），从而获得无自相互作用且更精确的泛函。

Method: 作者利用Levy自旋密度泛函理论的约束搜索，将Kohn-Sham方程中的电子密度约束为轨道密度依赖形式，并将总能量泛函构建为对轨道密度变化线性。具体地，将轨道φ_i[n]的KS乘法有效势v_s[n]约束为泛函v_eff[n-n_i]（即扣除该轨道自身密度的贡献），从而满足广义Koopmans条件。

Result: 该方法满足广义Koopmans条件，并相对于传统KS-DFT，将LDA和GGA在基本带隙上的平均绝对误差改善了约1 eV。

Abstract: The self-interaction error (SIE) of Kohn-Sham density functional theory (KS-DFT) leads to a non-physical, non-linear dependence of an orbital's energy on its own fractional occupation [Dabo et al., Phys. Rev. B, 82:115121, 2010]. The generalized Koopmans condition (GKC) ensures an atomic orbital's eigenenergy is independent of its fractional occupation$-$ and is free of self-interaction. The constrained search of the Levy spin-density-functional theory yields orbital densities and delivers the ground-state energy to generalize the Hohenberg-Kohn theorems. In this work, the electron density of the Kohn-Sham equations is constrained to be orbital-density dependent, with a total energy functional linear with respect to variation of its orbital densities. That is, the KS multiplicative effective potential $v_{\text{s}}[{n}]$ for an orbital $\varphi_i[{n}]$ is constrained to a functional $v_{\text{eff}}[{n - n_i}]$. The result complies with the generalized Koopmans condition (GKC). This approach improves LDA and GGA mean absolute errors (MAEs) in fundamental band gaps by $\sim$ 1 eV, with respect to KS-DFT. Equations free of electron self-interaction provide the basis for improvements in the accuracy, efficiency, and simplicity of density functional theories.

</details>

Here, we uncover an anomalous electron scattering mechanism from laterally correlated alloy disorder that spontaneously develops within the ScAlN barrier driven by strain-field undulations imposed by threading dislocations. Reducing the threading dislocation density from 2.8E10 to 4.4E8 cm^-2 suppresses areal density of nanodomains by more than two orders of magnitude. As a result, low-temperature Hall mobility increases from 1800 to 11995 cm^2*V^-1*s^-1. These findings establish dislocation-mediated Sc-rich nanodomains as the dominant source of anomalously strong alloy scattering, resolving the mobility bottleneck for high-performance nitride electronics.

The framework can also be extended to more complex ligands, including zwitterionic, multidentate, and polymerizable ligands, to explore a wider range of ligand-surface interactions and uncover new design principles for halide perovskite nanocrystals.

In contrast, the Berry curvature generates a complementary threefold Hall response governed by the cubic $A_{2g}$ Néel-order invariant. Although the Hall conductivity and the out-of-plane weak-ferromagnetic moment share the same symmetry and angular dependence, constraining the latter to zero leaves the optical Hall response essentially unchanged demonstrating that the Hall response originates directly from the compensated Néel order rather than the net magnetization. Localized Wannier orbitals reveal the bonding environments associated with this delocalization. In PbSe, pressure-induced band inversion is accompanied by an enhanced quantum metric and phonon softening without change in crystal symmetry and bonding. These results establish a common quantum geometric interpretation of bond delocalization and phonon softening across distinct bonding environments.

Abstract: Chiral systems can host nontrivial topological states, including characteristic edge modes and Fermi arcs. Here, we investigate the electronic properties of hexagonal NbSi$_2$ with chiral symmetry using first-principles calculations for both bulk and slab geometries. Furthermore, we demonstrate the existence of chiral edge modes with extended Fermi arcs around high-symmetry points. Abstract: We present \texttt{LetzElPhC}, an open-source software package that computes electron-phonon matrix elements from the output of density functional perturbation theory calculations performed with the \texttt{Quantum ESPRESSO} code. This symmetry-preserving approach avoids phase ambiguities and significantly reduces both computational costs and memory requirements. To support large-scale simulations on dense momentum grids, the package incorporates Fourier interpolation of dynamical matrices and perturbed Kohn-Sham potentials, accounting for long-range dipole and quadrupole interactions in both two- and three-dimensional materials, combined with multi-level parallelization and efficient parallel I/O. Together, these capabilities seamlessly complement the \texttt{Yambo} code to enable first-principles simulations of phonon-assisted optical spectroscopies, which we demonstrate through applications to resonant Raman scattering in monolayer $\text{WSe}_2$ and phonon-assisted luminescence in cubic AgBr.

Abstract: Compensated magnets provide a versatile platform for spin-dependent electronic and transport phenomena. Here, we show that magnetic reconfiguration, i.e., selective reversal of local moments without changing the underlying crystal structure, can profoundly transform the nonrelativistic spin splitting and spin-orbit-coupled responses of spin-degenerate antiferromagnets (AFMs), altermagnets (AMs), and compensated ferrimagnets (cFIMs). Strikingly, magnetic configurations with nearly degenerate energies can exhibit qualitatively different spin-split band structures and anomalous Hall responses, allowing a small magnetic-energy scale to produce large changes in physical properties. Combining tight-binding models, oriented spin space group analysis, and first-principles calculations, we demonstrate that converting an AM configuration into cFIM states activates staggered onsite-potential contributions to spin splitting, complementing the anisotropic-hopping mechanism of AMs and removing symmetry-enforced degeneracies. We demonstrate this regime in Mn2Mo3O8, one of 12 cFIM candidates identified in the MAGNDATA database, and further demonstrate its generality by designing cFIMs from collinear AFMs, exemplified by MnGeO3 and Co2Mo3O8. Our results establish magnetic reconfiguration as a powerful symmetry-guided strategy for dramatically reshaping electronic and transport responses in compensated magnets.

Abstract: An algorithm for finding the orientation of objects in an image that have a known rotational symmetry, the symmetry-adapted filter (SAF) algorithm, is presented. It is deterministic and training-free, which makes it fast and reproducible. A filter that encodes the known rotational symmetry and approximate peak width is centered on the symmetry center of the object of interest, masked to the region of interest, and rotated to maximize its inner product with the image. The approach is demonstrated on more than 20,000 small-angle neutron scattering images of a torque-driven skyrmion lattice. It is also demonstrated on atomically resolved real-space images, where mapping local atomic orientations enabled the identification of grains and grain boundaries and the quantification of lattice relaxation.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fe-Cr合金富Fe侧的fcc稳定区（γ环）形状异常：低温下Cr初始稳定fcc，但超过8 at.%后转为稳定bcc；高温下Cr始终稳定bcc。这一现象源于磁、振动与构型自由能贡献之间的微妙平衡，用第一性原理难以准确捕捉，因此需要一种能够平等处理这些因素的从头算方法。

Abstract: The region of fcc stability in Fe-rich Fe-Cr alloys, the $γ$-loop, has an anomalous shape: at low temperatures, Cr addition initially stabilizes fcc, but above 8 at.% Cr it stabilizes bcc. At high temperatures, Cr always stabilizes bcc. This behavior reflects a delicate balance between magnetic, vibrational and configurational free-energy contributions that is challenging to capture from first principles. We investigate phase stability in Fe1-xCrx (x = 0, 0.05, 0.10, 0.15) using coupled atomistic spin dynamics and ab initio molecular dynamics with thermodynamic integration. Gibbs free energy differences between fcc and bcc are calculated from 800 to 1800 K. Our calculations reproduce the experimental $γ$-loop and its compositional dependence, with near-quantitative accuracy for the $γ$-$δ$ transition. The $α$-$γ$ boundary, including its anomalous shape, is reproduced qualitatively, although transition temperatures are underestimated by approximately 200 K. Static calculations of magnetically ordered and disordered states provide an explanation: in ordered states, increasing Cr initially reduces the energetic preference for bcc over fcc, whereas in disordered states, Cr progressively stabilizes bcc at all compositions. Vibrational effects contribute to phase stability, while atomic short-range clustering at higher Cr content ($x \geq 0.15$) may explain the observed closing of the $γ$-loop from below. The simulations reveal finite Cr moments in the paramagnetic state, whose magnitudes depend on their local magnetic environment. The results demonstrate that the anomalous $γ$-loop arises from the interplay of magnetic energetics, magnetic disorder, lattice vibrations and chemical clustering, which must be treated on equal footing to capture phase stability from first principle

### [30] [Frozen Phonons on Dense $\mathbf{q}$-Grids Using Set Covers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34638)

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出一种确定最小非对角超胞集合的方法，使给定 q 网格上每个 q 点至少与一个超胞相称，从而大幅减少冻结声子法计算动力学矩阵所需的 DFT 计算量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冻结声子法求解动力学矩阵通常需要非常大的超胞，而非对角超胞法虽然能为每个 q 点构造最小相称超胞以减小尺寸，但要求为网格上每个 q 点分别建立新超胞，导致大量重复计算，因为同一非对角超胞可能与多个 q 点相称。

Method: 将问题形式化为寻找最小的超胞集合（集合覆盖问题），使网格上每个 q 点至少与其中一个超胞相称；作者提出一个求解算法并证明其给出最优解，随后以伪代码形式总结该算法，并讨论了超胞数量随网格密度的标度关系。

Result: 数值实验表明该方法在不同体系上均展现显著的计算优势，且优势随 q 网格密度的增加而增大；算法被证明能给出最优（最小）超胞集合。

Conclusion: 通过复用单个非对角超胞与多个 q 点的相称性，可显著降低计算成本，该方法随网格加密收益更大，为高效声子计算提供了可靠的超胞选择方案。

Abstract: Phonon dynamics determine a vast number of properties in the solid state, such as the heat capacity, thermal expansion, and thermal conductivity. Phonons are obtained through the dynamical matrix, which, in the frozen phonon approach, is calculated as a finite difference derivative of the analytical Hellmann-Feynman force. This approach unfortunately typically suffers from requiring very large supercells, even for modest $\mathbf{q}$-grids. The non-diagonal supercell method remedies this issue by determining the smallest supercell commensurate with a given $\mathbf{q}$-point, drastically reducing the supercell size, but in turn requiring a new supercell for each $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid. This introduces redundancy, however, since a single non-diagonal supercell may be commensurate with several $\mathbf{q}$-points. In this work I present a method to determine a minimal set of supercells, such that every $\mathbf{q}$-point on the grid is commensurate with at least one supercell. I present an algorithm to solve the problem and prove that it yields the optimal solution, before summarizing the algorithm using pseudocode. The scaling of the number of supercells with the density of the grid is discussed. Numerical results on a variety of systems show the significant computational advantage of the method, which increases with the density of the grid.

Abstract: Anomalous transverse shifts in interface reflection are conventionally attributed to spin-orbit coupling or its pseudospin analogue. Here, we uncover a distinct geometric mechanism driven solely by an orbital torque arising from Fermi-surface anisotropy. During total reflection, an incident wave packet penetrates the anisotropic transmitted medium as an evanescent wave. There, the broken rotational symmetry about the interface normal exerts an orbital torque, and the accumulated angular impulse---the product of the torque and the dwell time---leads to a transverse spatial shift. Applying this mechanism to altermagnets without spin-orbit coupling, we show that the orbital torque is odd in spin. As a consequence, the two spin channels are deflected to opposite sides of the incident plane, giving an intrinsic spatial spin separation in altermagnets. The shift also inherits the angular symmetry of the altermagnetic spin splitting and is strongly enhanced near the onset of total reflection. Our work thus uncovers a new mechanism for transverse shifts, predicts a novel spin separation effect in altermagnets, and offers a direct probe of the altermagnetic order.

Abstract: Air-sensitive chalcogenide films are routinely protected by a capping layer, which must then be removed, or contacted through, before electronic transport can be measured, destroying the surface the cap was meant to preserve. A Te-on-GaAs witness shows the cap to be optically indistinguishable from bare substrate, transmitting 1.00(4) of the substrate amplitude between 0.2 and 1.5 THz, which limits the error from neglecting it to 3.6 % for the 90 nm film, although the same estimate reaches 48 % at 25 nm. In this matched set, the capped films form an ordered series, monotonic in thickness in both conductance magnitude and lineshape, whereas the uncapped films do not. The capping layer therefore presents no obstacle to terahertz characterization, and in this set it coincides with a more reproducible film response.

Abstract: The aim of this study is to determine how the class and asymptotic behaviour of a Volterra memory kernel influence relaxation in a coupled thermomechanical model. Abstract: We study the space of fundamental symmetries of a real or complex Krein space in finite or infinite dimension. Its canonical connection is the tangential projection of the flat derivative in the real Banach space of Krein-self-adjoint operators. This yields explicit formulas for geodesics, parallel transport, transvections, and curvature. The positive square roots $(JJ')^{1/2}$ implement both isometries between the associated Hilbert spaces and geodesic transvections. A proof of their intertwining property in an earlier publication is corrected.

Abstract: We prove a quantitative error bound for a fixed-axis variant of the transverse spin-gradient approximation (TSGA) in noncollinear spin density functional theory. Inspired by Eich and Gross, we use a spin-spiral wave (SSW) as the local reference for the grand-canonical Levy-Lieb energy with the Hartree term subtracted. We construct the thermodynamic limit of the electron gas with constant total density and a prescribed SSW magnetization. Its energy per unit volume $e_{\rm SSW}$ is evaluated at the local wavevector $\nabla\varphi$, where $\varphi$ is the spiral phase. When $\varphi$ is the azimuthal angle of the magnetization $\mathbf{m}=(m_1,m_2,m_3)$, the approximation reads \[
  E(ρ,\mathbf{m})\approx\int_{\mathbb{R}^3}e_{\rm SSW}\big(ρ(\mathbf{x}),\mathbf{m}(\mathbf{x}),\nabla\varphi(\mathbf{x})\big)\,d \mathbf{x}. \] Here $\nabla\varphi$ measures the local rotation of $\mathbf{m}$ about the third axis. Wherever $m_1^2+m_2^2>0$, it equals $(m_1\nabla m_2-m_2\nabla m_1)/(m_1^2+m_2^2)$. The approximation thus depends only on the total density $ρ$, the magnetization and its first derivatives. When the spin-polarized density in the rotating frame and the local wavevector vary on the length scale $N^{1/3}$, with $N$ the mean number of particles, we obtain an error $O(N^{5/6})$ for an energy of order $N$. Setting the phase to zero gives the noncollinear local spin density approximation (LSDA) for arbitrary magnetization directions.

Abstract: Gradient-based training of a parametric model is the motion of a damped particle in the potential set by the loss. Using the Bateman-Galley doubling of the degrees of freedom, which gives dissipative systems an action principle, we write gradient flow, the heavy-ball method and Nesterov's accelerated gradient method as Hamiltonian systems. We show that, within this Hamiltonian description, backpropagation is Dirac evolution. The activations of a feed-forward network are treated as coordinates of phase space, and the layer equations as constraints. These constraints are second class for every architecture, and Hamilton's equations with the resulting Dirac bracket reproduce training with the exact gradient of the loss, although the Hamiltonian contains only the cost of the network output. The Dirac bracket of an activation with the momenta of the parameters is its Jacobian, the inverse of the constraint matrix contains both the forward and the reverse modes of automatic differentiation, and the errors propagated by backpropagation are the Lagrange multipliers of the layer constraints: the constraint forces of a kinematic chain under the load of the cost. These results hold in the full doubled phase space, not only in the physical limit. The network's part of the constraint algebra is the same for every optimizer, Nesterov's look-ahead adds one layer of momentum-dependent constraints that couple the two copies of the doubled system, and gradient flow is obtained by constructing the Dirac bracket in stages. We confirm these statements numerically to machine precision and train a network by integrating the Dirac flow of its constraints, with no backward pass in the computation.

</details>

Abstract: We derive the Hamiltonian formulation of the Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (KP) equation from a singular Lagrangian by means of the Dirac-Bergmann algorithm. We confirm these results by the Faddeev-Jackiw method. Then, we consider fields periodic in the first spatial coordinate. In this case, the zero modes of the derivative operator lead to an additional secondary constraint on the mean of the potential. This constraint replaces the inverse derivative by its zero mean counterpart, and the resulting Hamiltonian is in accordance with the one of Reiman and Semenov-Tyan-Shanskii, up to normalization conventions. We also relate our results to an existing quantization of the KP equation, for which the potential Lagrangian provides an action.

Abstract: We construct the black hole infrared triangle of scalar QED on a stretched Schwarzschild horizon at tree level. In the covariant phase space of the near-horizon Maxwell field, the soft charge is a corner term. The memory is the zero mode of the gauge-invariant radiative field along the generators. It is conjugate to the boundary gauge mode that labels the degenerate horizon vacua, and Gauss' law fixes it by the flux of charge through the horizon. The Noether flux of the residual gauge transformation through the stretched horizon is not conserved by the scattering, because charged matter crosses the stretched horizon in both directions. The flux with its matter part removed is the soft charge, which generates the large gauge transformation. Its Ward identity is equivalent to a soft photon theorem in every radiative partial wave, which we derive from the near-horizon Feynman rules and, where these do not control the emission, from the field equations at leading order. The main difference from null infinity is that on a stretched horizon both radial branches of the field are independent boundary data. The hard charge is a radial flux. With both branches present it weights each leg by the direction of its radial motion, and the soft factor is a sign per leg. The endpoint data on the two branches give two boundary observables, the time-integrated transverse magnetic and electric fields. The field radiated by a scattering process contributes only to the first, where it cancels the change in the Coulomb memory of the charges, so that the soft charge is conserved. A charge that crosses the horizon enters both. We also discuss the infrared divergence, the dressing of the external states, and the fate of the memory in the Hartle--Hawking state, where it performs a random walk driven by the Hawking radiation.

Abstract: The surface impedance of a body is fixed by its interior alone and encodes its entire tidal response. The extension to include minimally coupled gravity is straightforward. Examples are provided, including constructing the Dirac equation in spherical and cylindrical symmetry (with or without gravity) and the Kerr spacetime. This manuscript should be accessible to readers who have studied the Dirac equation at an introductory level (e.g., in Cartesian coordinates in flat spacetime with constant gamma matrices). The emphasis is on the use of orthonormal frames, since these correspond to the coordinate systems most familiar to readers when dealing with most field theories, and represent the physical frames where measurements are performed. Abstract: We compute vacuum tripartite Rényi multi-entropy in a two-dimensional compact-boson conformal field theory (CFT). An independent calculation of the partition function on a Fermat curve yields the adjacent three-point coefficient. In the fusion limit, the Riemann surface for disjoint intervals degenerates into two Fermat curves, and the residue of the four-point function reproduces the squared three-point coefficient, as required by the operator product expansion. Conclusion: 高阶导数与幺正性破坏之间并无必然联系，"良性鬼场"系统真实存在；四维高阶导数引力的鬼场性质仍悬而未决，但其经典极限下可视为良性；超对称情形中，共形超引力的鬼场可能良性，而六维可重整规范理论则因鬼场非良性而不幺正。

Abstract: We outline the history of the research of dynamic systems including higher derivatives in the Lagrangian, The story starts in 1744 when Euler published a book where the Euler--Lagrange differential equations for a HD system were first written. The next important landmarks were the papers of Ostrogradsky (1850) and of Pais and Uhlenbeck (1950). Many articles written in the second half of the last century were influenced by a misconception that was widespread at the time --- it was believed that higher derivatives necessarily bring about a violation of unitarity.
  We understand today that this is not the case and there are many systems with benign ghosts where HD are present but unitarity is not violated. In this review, we present and discuss several examples of such (quantum) mechanical systems and 2D field theories. Then we will briefly discuss higher-derivative gravity theories in 4 dimensions. In that case, the question whether the ghosts there are benign or not has a special status due to the absence of a nonperturbative Hamiltonian and does not have a definite answer yet. However, if one restricts oneself to classical theory, one can argue that the ghosts {\it are} benign (benign classical ghostly system is a system that needs an infinite time to reach a singularity, and it seems to the case both in ordinary and in HD gravity: the cosmic censorship conjecture implies that the singularities are always surrounded by horizons, and it takes an infinite time on the clocks of a distant observer to reach a horizon).
  Finally, I discuss supersymmetric HD theories: conformal supergravity, where the ghosts are also probably benign, and then an interesting supersymmetric HD gauge theory in 6 dimensions. It involves a dimensionless coupling constant, is renormalizable and rather beautiful. Unfortunately, it is not unitary --- ghosts are not benign there.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清二维 Toda 共形场论中半退化原初场的晚期 OTOC 行为，修正 Liouville 理论中此前报道的常数极限，并检验更高秩（N≥4）情形下相图的适用性。

Conclusion: N=2、3 的相图不能推广到 N≥4，更高秩情形需要额外贡献；Liouville 理论的 OTOC 衰减律应修正为 t^{-3/2}。

Abstract: We study late-time out-of-time-order correlators (OTOCs) of semi-degenerate primary fields in two-dimensional $A_{N-1}$ Toda conformal field theory. In Liouville theory ($N=2$), we find that the OTOC decays as $t^{-3/2}$ when the continuum edge dominates, correcting a previously reported constant limit. For $N=3$, a transverse contour pinch reduces the spectral integral to one dimension. Assuming that the reduced expansion is complete, in the continuous phase the regulated edge behaves as a discrete state, and the OTOC approaches a pure braiding phase. As we check numerically, the edge block is a free-field correlator in a charge-neutral Weyl frame; this explains the pure phase and is consistent with crossing symmetry. For both $N=2$ and $N=3$, a dominant discrete residue gives a constant late-time limit. For $N\geq4$, the same one-dimensional reduction violates a crossing bound on the lowest intermediate weight of a positive diagonal expansion. Hence the $N=2,3$ phase diagram does not extend to higher rank without additional contributions below the reduced continuum edge or outside a positive diagonal expansion.

Abstract: We resolve a puzzle in the computation of holographic correlators using supergravity when defects are present. When the radiation is assigned to ADM mass loss at fixed smearing length $\ell=4\sqrt{Θ/π}$, the result is $\dot S_{\rm HBAR}\simeq\dot A_p/[4(1-\ell/r_+)]$. The mass dependence of the exterior source thus enters the entropy per unit area lost at order $\sqrtΘ$.

Abstract: We show that the holographic spectral function of a neutral massless scalar on the extremal Reissner-Nordstrom black brane in $AdS_5$ admits an elegant formula in terms of the quasi-normal modes (QNMs) and branch-cut data. In the extremal case, the spectral data comprises the zeros of the boundary source function, i.e. the QNMs, as well as its phase discontinuity across the branch cut on the negative imaginary axis of the complex frequency plane. This formula neatly factorizes into an infinite product over the QNM poles and a factor encoding the branch cut discontinuity. We derive this pole-cut formula as the zero-temperature limit of the QNM product formula for holographic thermal correlators, with the branch-cut contribution arising from the coalescence of the QNMs on the negative imaginary axis. We also clarify the role of the near-horizon $AdS_2$ region in this context. Consistency with the OPE of the boundary correlator provides extremal sum-rules involving both the QNMs as well as the phase discontinuity function. We also find that this holographic consistency forces the asymptotic form of the QNMs to be related to the asymptotics of the phase discontinuity function. We verify our formula and these OPE constraints numerically. We also demonstrate that, in the time domain, the pole and cut factors each produce complex-time singularities, whose location is determined by the optical distance between the boundary and the black-brane singularity.   <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We revisit a long-standing problem of correlation functions with the Harish-Chandra-Itzykson-Zuber measure. The immediate goal is to reproduce the long-known answer for pair correlator in the form of character expansion - a sum over Schur polynomials with carefully adjusted coefficients. The infinite sum can be folded up into exponentials with the help of the Cauchy identity. What matters are the coefficients, which we express in terms of projectors, made from Cherednik operators. Development of character expansion methods for unitary integrals, which are much more difficult than the Hermitian ones, gives a hope for further generalizations - up to arbitrary correlators with non-trivial measures.

</details>

Abstract: Energy correlators are infrared safe because each detector weights a parton by its energy, but a weight in the arrival time makes them infrared unsafe in gauge theories. The logarithm does not come from a single soft gluon, but from a soft parton pair with comparable energies, one parton in each detector; its coefficient is a closed-form function of the angle for any matter content. In $N=4$ super Yang-Mills theory, we recover this coefficient from the two-gluon Regge term of the known four-point function and derive a simple relation to the logarithm in the charge correlator. ### [90] [Pairing from anti-Hund's coupling under infinite Hubbard repulsion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33286)
*Donggyu Kim,Ya-Hui Zhang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了在强关联材料中，当库仑排斥U占主导、声子介导相互作用很弱时，反洪德耦合J_A（通过光学声子介导的轨道间自旋交换）如何产生非常规超导。作者在U=∞极限下，将三角晶格上掺杂的双轨道Hubbard模型约化为四味t-J_A模型，并用DMRG证明在1≤J_A/|t|≤10范围内存在稳健的配对，提出了超越BCS的强耦合超导图像，并讨论了对魔角扭曲双层石墨烯的可能应用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 核心问题是：在强关联材料中，声子介导的配对相互作用通常远弱于在位Hubbard排斥U，那么当U占主导时声子机制是否以及如何仍能产生超导配对。作者通过引入反洪德耦合J_A这一最小模型来回答这一基本问题。

Finally, we discuss possible applications to magic-angle twisted bilayer graphene.

Rev. Lett. \textbf{133}, 036501 (2024)]. We proposed that these vibronic states order cooperatively and thereby explain the hidden quadrupole and canted magnetic phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ [Phys. Rev. B \textbf{107}, L220404 (2023)], but that proposal rested on a simplified model. Here, we show that a spin-vibronic Hamiltonian derived from first principles, with a single scale factor for the elastic coupling, reproduces the ordered phases of Ba$_2$MgReO$_6$ with the same vibronic states that describe its RIXS spectra. With this factor bringing the quadrupolar transition to 34.7 K, near the measured 33 K, the theory predicts the magnetic transition at 19.3 K, against the measured 18 K, and reproduces the antiferro distortion and the ferro elongation between the transitions. Abstract: Saturated ferromagnetism has been proven for every on-site repulsion $U > 0$ in Hubbard models with a flat lowest band, and the interaction required for it grows with the band width $W$ when the band becomes dispersive. For the chain, a subspace of only $3\times 10^{-5}$ of the full configuration space gives a state with more than $97\%$ of its weight in the sector of maximal spin. For the decorated square lattice of $4\times 3$ unit cells on 48 qubits, the computed states in subspaces of less than $1\%$ of the full configuration space switch from low-spin states to nearly fully polarized states at $U \simeq 2W$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 魔角扭曲双层石墨烯在ν=-2附近的KIVC相已被广泛确立，但近期超净样品实验报道了TIVC相的特征，作者希望从理论上探究IVC相金属区域内是否存在隐藏的TIVC序，并系统刻画随载流子密度调谐的多重相变。

Method: 作者在填充因子ν=-2到ν=-3范围内改变载流子密度，并通过改变栅极距离来调节库仑相互作用强度，结合理论计算分析各相的自由能竞争、序参量演化和费米面重构。

Result: 结果表明：ν=-2附近的KIVC相内隐藏着f波时间反演对称的能谷间相干（TIVC）序，其强度在远离ν=-2的IVC金属区内逐渐增强。随着载流子密度降低，系统经历多重相变：IVC相先通过类二级相变转变为能谷极化相，随后因亚晶格极化的逐步发展进入量子反常霍尔（QAH）相，各相变伴随费米面的大幅重构。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上为近期实验观测到的TIVC特征提供了支持，并揭示了魔角扭曲双层石墨烯在ν=-2到ν=-3之间丰富的相变序列（KIVC→能谷极化→QAH）及其与费米面重构的关联。

Abstract: We study the ground state phase diagram of twisted bilayer graphene at magic angle as function of carrier density and the Coulomb interaction. The carrier density is varied between the fillings $ν=-2$ and $ν=-3$ and the Coulomb interaction is tuned by changing the gate distance. Our results reveal that the well established Kramers intervalley coherent (KIVC) phase near $ν=-2$, contains a hidden $f$-wave time-reversal symmetric intervalley coherent (TIVC) order, whose strength gradually increases within the metallic regime of the intervalley coherent (IVC) phase away from $ν=-2$. This finding is interesting in the light of a recent experimental work [26], where the signatures of the TIVC phase has been reported in ultra-clean samples. We further show that the system goes through multiple phase transitions as the carrier density is tuned. Near the $ν=-2$, the IVC phase is the ground state, which gives way to valley polarized phase through a second-order-like phase transition as the carrier density is lowered. Further tuning of density pushes the system into the quantum anomalous Hall phase through gradual development of the sublattice polarization. These phase transitions are accompanied by major reconstructions of the Fermi contours.

</details>


### [98] [Fractional Chern Insulator in a Finite-Range One-Band Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34255)
*Tong Liu,Hoi Chun Po*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 作者通过系统“平凡化”单粒子量子几何，构造了一个方格子上仅含四体元胞内相互作用、无任何单粒子拓扑背景的单能带纯相互作用模型，并在三分之一填充下发现分数陈绝缘体（FCI）态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上实现分数陈绝缘体依赖工程化能带以模仿朗道能级，从而提供单粒子拓扑背景。本研究旨在探究在完全没有单粒子量子几何的情况下，FCI 是否仍能仅由相互作用涌现。

Method: 通过对超越朗道能级范式的 FCI 模型进行单粒子量子几何的系统平凡化，构造出方格子上单能带、纯相互作用的模型，所有作用项都局限于单个元胞内；再利用精确对角化（ED）和密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）在三分之一填充下进行计算，并分析两粒子排斥扇区中的拓扑性质。

Result: 模型表现出三重低能流形、霍尔电导为 -2e²/(3h)，以及不破坏平移对称性的分数电荷泵浦现象。该行为可追溯至排斥性两粒子扇区中的非平庸拓扑。

Conclusion: 分数陈绝缘体可以在完全无单粒子量子几何的纯相互作用模型中涌现，其分数量子霍尔序源自两粒子扇区的非平庸拓扑，为强关联起源提供了微观视角。

Abstract: A common route for realizing fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) is to engineer bands that mimic the physics of Landau levels, which serve as the single-particle topological background for the emergence of strongly correlated fractional quantum Hall states. Here we show that FCIs can also arise in a one-band model devoid of any single-particle quantum geometry. Through a systematic trivialization of the single-particle quantum geometry in FCI models that go beyond the Landau-level paradigm, we construct a single-band, interaction-only model on the square lattice, with all terms confined to individual plaquettes. At one-third filling, exact diagonalization and density-matrix renormalization-group calculations reveal a three-state low-energy manifold, a Hall conductance of $-2e^2/(3h)$, and fractional charge pumping without translation-symmetry breaking. We trace this behavior to nontrivial topology in the repulsive two-particle sector, offering a microscopic perspective on the strongly correlated origin of fractional quantum Hall order.

</details>


### [99] [Spin-Orbit Coupling in Symmetry-Forbidden Reactions: A Selection Rule Tested on the Chalcogen Trimers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34375)
*Shadan Ghassemi Tabrizi*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一条基于对称性的规则：在自旋-轨道耦合作用下，两个轨道（旋量）只有当其差异对应于一个旋转的对称性时才能混合，且该对称性的算符分量耦合了基态与双自由基三重态。作者以硫族三聚体（O3 至 Po3）的开环反应为测试，证明规则所指定的算符分量单独作用即可显著降低反应势垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的轨道对称性守恒（Woodward-Hoffmann）规则在重元素体系中因自旋-轨道耦合而失效。作者旨在建立并检验一条能涵盖自旋-轨道耦合的对称性规则，以理解重元素体系中被对称性禁阻反应如何变得允许，并定量评估各算符分量对势垒降低的贡献。

Method: 以硫族三聚体 O3 至 Po3 的开环反应为模型体系，采用 NEVPT2 与自旋-轨道组态相互作用（SO-CI）方法，在 C2v 势能面上研究面内与面外轨道交叉。让自旋-轨道耦合算符的三个分量分别独立作用，比较规则指定分量与其他分量对势垒的影响，并考察三重态与基态的交叉行为。

Result: 在 Se3 和 Te3 中，规则指定的算符分量单独作用即可将势垒降低 2.2 和 5.1 kcal/mol（完整算符为 2.2 和 5.2 kcal/mol），其他两个分量仅贡献 0.2 和 1.3 kcal/mol；在 Po3 中它们以二阶方式贡献。三重态在禁阻过渡态下方 6.5–9.0 kcal/mol 处与基态交叉。自旋-轨道耦合使该交叉在 O3 和 S3 中变得可通过，从 Se3 起将两个态合并为单一表面，其鞍点分别降低 2.6、5.5 和 13.1 kcal/mol。分子转动服从同一规则但强度弱 6 至 2.8×10³ 倍。

Conclusion: 该对称性规则在硫族三聚体系列中得到验证：规则指定的自旋-轨道耦合算符分量主导了对称性禁阻反应的势垒降低，且三重态与基态的交叉被耦合打开，使禁阻反应变得可行。分子转动遵循同一规则但耦合强度远弱于自旋-轨道耦合，这一点与环状臭氧的寿命预测相关。

Abstract: A reaction is forbidden by orbital symmetry when an occupied and an empty orbital of different symmetry change places along its path. With spin-orbit coupling orbitals become spinors, and those of two such orbitals can mix only if the orbitals differ by the symmetry of a rotation; the operator component of that symmetry couples ground state and diradical triplet. We test this rule on the ring opening of the chalcogen trimers O3 to Po3, where an in-plane and an out-of-plane orbital cross ($C_{2v}$ surfaces; NEVPT2, spin-orbit configuration interaction), by letting the operator components act separately. In Se3 and Te3 the component named by the rule alone lowers the barrier by 2.2 and 5.1 kcal/mol (complete operator: 2.2 and 5.2), the other two by 0.2 and 1.3 kcal/mol; in Po3 they contribute in second order. The triplet crosses the ground state 6.5 to 9.0 kcal/mol below the forbidden saddle. Spin-orbit coupling makes this crossing passable in O3 and S3 and, from Se3 on, merges the two states into one surface whose saddle lies 2.6, 5.5, and 13.1 kcal/mol lower. Molecular rotation obeys the same rule but is 6 to $2.8\times 10^{3}$ times weaker, which bears on the lifetime predicted for cyclic ozone.

</details>


### [100] [Signatures of multiorbital density waves in the electronic Raman response of bilayer nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34471)
*Lauro B. Braz,Ge He,Luis G. G. V. Dias da Silva,Ilya M. Eremin*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文建立一个双轨道双层模型分析低压双层镍酸盐在自旋密度波(SDW)态下的电子拉曼响应，发现不同对称通道(B1g/B2g)的拉曼峰对应不同的多轨道能量尺度，无法单一等同于某个SDW能隙，从而为La3Ni2O7的拉曼特征提供统一解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期双层镍酸盐的拉曼散射实验在SDW态中观测到显著谱特征，但其微观起源存在争议：一种解释为双磁振子激发，另一种解释为电子跨越SDW诱导能隙的跃迁。作者旨在厘清这些拉曼特征的真实来源，并厘清轨道对SDW序参量的贡献。

Method: 采用包含正交结构中d_{3z²-r²}与d_{x²-y²}两个轨道的双层模型，分析多轨道无自旋双条形(double stripe)SDW态的电子拉曼响应，并通过对称性分解考察B1g与B2g通道的谱特征。

Result: B2g通道在能量尺度(M_{z²}+M_{x²})/2处出现峰，源于弱杂化带的SDW能隙打开；而B1g通道的主峰出现在多轨道能,(M_{z²}²+M_{x²}²)^{1/2}处，反映相关电子态的强杂化特征。这两个不同能量尺度可统一解释La3Ni2O7中多种拉曼特征。

Conclusion: SDW态(与超导态不同)的拉曼峰位一般不能简单等同于单一SDW能隙；对称性分辨的电子拉曼散射可作为探测多轨道密度波序的灵敏手段，并可用于区分不同轨道对SDW序参量的贡献。

Abstract: Recent Raman scattering experiments on bilayer nickelates have revealed pronounced spectral features in the spin-density-wave state, whose microscopic origin remains controversial, with interpretations ranging from two-magnon excitations to electronic transitions across SDW-induced gaps. Here, we demonstrate that the multiorbital electronic structure of low-pressure bilayer nickelates naturally generates distinct orbital-selective Raman signatures in different symmetry channels. Using a two-orbital bilayer model incorporating the $d_{3z^2-r^2}$ and $d_{x^2-y^2}$ orbitals in the orthorhombic structure, we analyze the electronic Raman response of the multiorbital spin-spinless double stripe SDW state and show that the $B_{2g}$ response exhibits a peak at the energy scale $(M_{z^2}+M_{x^2})/2$, associated with SDW gap openings in weakly hybridized bands. In contrast, the dominant peak in the $B_{1g}$ channel emerges at the multiorbital energy scale $(M_{z^2}^2+M_{x^2}^2)^{1/2}$, reflecting the strongly hybridized character of the relevant electronic states. These distinct energy scales provide a unified interpretation of several Raman signatures observed in La$_3$Ni$_2$O$_7$ and offer a means of disentangling the orbital contributions to the SDW order parameters. Our results highlight that in contrast to the superconducting state, Raman peak positions in the SDW state cannot generally be identified with a single SDW gap and establish symmetry-resolved electronic Raman scattering as a sensitive probe of multiorbital density-wave order.

</details>


### [101] [Emergent Chern metal in strongly correlated moiré bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34780)
*Kaushal Kumar Kesharpu,Evgenii A. Kochetov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了强关联莫尔过渡金属硫族化合物（TMD）双层中，自旋-电荷分离态与层极化螺旋自旋织构耦合的拓扑性质。研究发现非共面自旋子背景导致能带显著不对称翘曲，使全局间接带隙在大部分参数空间内闭合，真正的拓扑绝缘体仅存在于精调临界轨迹上；但有限的局域直接带隙持续存在，稳定了宽广的拓扑陈金属相（C=±1）。该相自然产生可观但非量子化的反常霍尔响应，并伴随有限纵向电导，为莫尔体系中的耗散性反常霍尔态提供了纯电子强耦合机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索莫尔 TMD 双层中自旋-电荷分离态与螺旋自旋织构耦合下的拓扑性质，寻找在强关联背景下可能出现的新型拓扑电子态及其输运表现。

Method: 针对在非共面自旋子背景中运动的无自旋空穴子（holon）构建有效的低能哈密顿量，分析其能带结构，计算陈数（Chern number）与态密度（density of states），进而评估反常霍尔响应与纵向电导行为。

Result: 能带呈现显著的不对称翘曲，导致全局间接带隙在大部分参数空间内闭合，真正的拓扑绝缘体仅局限于精调过的临界轨迹；然而有限的局域直接带隙仍然存在，从而稳定了大范围的陈金属相（C=±1），并产生非量子化但可观的反常霍尔响应与有限纵向电导。

Conclusion: 自旋-电荷分离态与层极化螺旋自旋织构的耦合通过能带翘曲抑制了全局拓扑绝缘体的形成，却稳定了拓扑陈金属相，为莫尔体系中耗散性反常霍尔态提供了一种纯电子的强耦合机制。

Abstract: We investigate the topological properties of a strongly correlated moiré transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) bilayer (such as twisted $\mathrm{MoTe}_2$ or $\mathrm{WSe}_2$) hosting a spin-charge separated state coupled to a layer-polarized helical spin texture. Analyzing an effective low-energy Hamiltonian for mobile spinless holons moving through this non-coplanar spinon background, we reveal pronounced asymmetric band warping. This warping closes the global indirect band gap across most of the parameter space, restricting true topological insulators to fine-tuned critical trajectories. However, a finite local direct gap persists, stabilizing broad regions of a topological Chern metal ($C = \pm 1$). Computing the Chern number and density of states, we demonstrate that this phase naturally yields a sizable, non-quantized anomalous Hall response accompanied by finite longitudinal conduction, providing a purely electronic strong-coupling mechanism for dissipative anomalous Hall states in moiré systems.

</details>


### [102] [Spin Loop Currents and Bulk-Edge Structure in Correlated Topological States of WSe$_2$/MoTe$_2$ Bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34880)
*Po-Jui Chen,Kevin P. Lucht,Andreas Gleis,Andrew J. Millis,J. H. Pixley,Daniele Guerci*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文基于部分子（parton）平均场理论，研究 AB 堆叠 WSe2/MoTe2 双层在 ν=2 填充下由 MoTe2 层载流子间的非局域相互作用驱动的拓扑近藤半金属（TKSM）到拓扑近藤绝缘体（TKI）的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受近期实验启发，需要从理论上理解 AB 堆叠 WSe2/MoTe2 双层中价涨落、局域与扩展 Hubbard 相互作用（尤其是 MoTe2 层载流子间的非局域相互作用）对拓扑物态的影响。

Method: 采用部分子平均场框架，将价涨落、局域与长程 Hubbard 相互作用纳入理论；并分析伊辛自旋轨道耦合与非局域电子关联相互作用产生的涌现自旋回路电流，进一步计算其对边缘输运的影响。

Result: MoTe2 层载流子间的非局域相互作用可使体系在填充 ν=2 处从拓扑近藤半金属（TKSM）转变到拓扑近藤绝缘体（TKI），相变源于伊辛自旋轨道耦合与非局域电子关联共同产生的涌现自旋回路电流；该自旋回路电流使两端电导在特定偏压下取量子化值。

Conclusion: 非局域相互作用在 MoTe2 层中扮演关键角色，通过涌现自旋回路电流驱动 TKSM–TKI 拓扑相变，并在边缘输运中表现为量子化的两端电导。

Abstract: Motivated by recent experiments, we theoretically investigate AB-stacked WSe$_2$/MoTe$_2$ bilayers. Our theory incorporates valence fluctuations, local and extended (longer-range) Hubbard interactions, including non-local interactions between carriers on the MoTe$_2$ layer. Within the parton mean-field framework, we show that the non-local interaction between MoTe$_2$ carriers can drive a transition between a topological Kondo semimetal(TKSM) and a topological Kondo insulator(TKI) at the filling fraction $ν=2$. We attribute this transition to an emergent spin-loop current arising from the interplay between Ising spin-orbit coupling and non-local electron correlations. In addition, we investigate how the emergent spin-loop current affects edge transport and show that the two-point conductance takes a quantized value at certain bias voltages.

</details>


### [103] [A microscopic continuum model for moiré transition metal dichalcogenides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.34937)
*Julian Ingham,Harshitra Mahalingam,Raquel Queiroz*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文给出一个微观推导的连续模型，通过保留单层价带和导带，以与角度无关的参数精确描述扭曲过渡金属二硫化物（tTMDs）在不同扭转角下的能带结构，并解释了近理想量子几何、异常朗道能级斜率和层间电荷重分布等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: tTMDs 是研究多体相（如分数陈绝缘体和超导）的重要平台，但现有单轨道模型无法准确描述其能带结构、量子几何及轨道磁性等性质，需要构建一个角度无关、能够统一描述不同扭转角的微观连续模型。

Method: 作者从微观出发推导连续模型，同时保留单层价带和导带，引入通过 AB 堆叠区域的隧穿路径；将这些导带路径下折（downfold）到价带后，得到与扭转角可调谐的层间耦合。模型被证明是带魔角的手征模型的微扰。

Result: 该模型以角度无关的参数准确描述了一系列扭转角下的能带结构；解释了莫尔能带近理想的量子几何；与单轨道理论相比，预言了显著不同的轨道磁矩（表现为异常的朗道能级斜率）以及层间电荷的重分布，从而改变了莫尔铁电势。

Conclusion: 通过保留价带和导带并下折，所构建的角度无关连续模型能更准确地刻画 tTMDs 的能带与物理性质，揭示了近理想量子几何的起源，并修正了单轨道理论对轨道磁矩和莫尔铁电势的预测。

Abstract: Twisted transition metal dichalcogenides (tTMDs) have emerged as a fascinating platform for many-body phases --- displaying fractional Chern insulators and superconductivity as a function of doping. Here, we give a microscopic derivation of a continuum model which accurately describes their bandstructure across a range of twist angles with angle-independent parameters. This angle-independent description rests on retaining both the monolayer valence and conduction bands, which introduce tunnelling paths through AB-stacked regions; upon downfolding to the valence band, these paths supply a substantial twist-tunable contribution to the interlayer coupling. We show that the model is a perturbation of a chiral model with a magic angle, explaining the near-ideal quantum geometry of the moiré bands. The model further produces a strongly different orbital magnetic moment to the single-orbital theory --- predicting an anomalous Landau level slope --- and a redistribution of charge between the layers, modifying the moiré ferroelectric potential.

</details>

### [104] [Spontaneous Time-Reversal-Symmetry-Breaking State in Proximity-Induced Superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33288)
*Jonathan Schirmer,Wei Pan,Javad Shabani,Enrico Rossi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文提出一个最小机制，解释在s波超导体与Dirac半金属构成的异质结中，无需磁序或非常规配对即可自发破缺时间反演对称性的超导态，并给出其存在条件、基态性质与相图，部分情形还展现出重入行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验表明，s波超导体与Dirac半金属异质结中的超导态可破缺时间反演对称性，尽管各组分既无磁序也无需非常规配对。作者旨在为该现象提供一个无需引入磁性或非常规配对的极简理论机制。

Method: 作者考虑了与常规s波超导体邻近耦合的多带系统，推导出破缺时间反演对称性超导态出现的条件，并证明该态在存在时即为基态。随后计算并得到了完整相图。

Result: 研究发现该时间反演破缺态在其存在区域始终为基态；部分相图中出现重入行为，即连续改变某一参数可使系统在时间反演对称态与自发破缺时间反演对称态之间发生多次转变。

Conclusion: 该结果有助于识别并工程化设计异质结，使其在无外磁场或磁化的条件下实现非互易超导输运效应。

Abstract: Recent experiments on superconducting heterostructures formed by an $s$-wave superconductor and a Dirac semimetal suggest that in these systems the superconducting state can break time-reversal symmetry even though no magnetic order or unconventional pairing is present in any of the structure's components. We present a minimal mechanism for a time-reversal-symmetry-breaking superconducting state in multiband systems proximity-coupled to a conventional $s$-wave superconductor. We derive the conditions for this state, find that it is the ground state wherever it exists, and obtain the phase diagrams. We find that in some situations the phase diagrams describe reentrant behavior: by continuously varying a parameter the system can undergo multiple transitions between a time-reversal-symmetric state and a state that spontaneously breaks time-reversal symmetry. The results help identify heterostructures that can be engineered to obtain nonreciprocal superconducting transport effects even when no magnetic field or magnetization is present.

</details>


### [105] [Spectroscopic Signatures of Polaronic Density Wave Fluctuations in Superconducting Bismuthates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33422)
*Wojciech R. Pudełko,Muntaser Naamneh,Philip M. Dee,Hang Li,Eduardo B. Guedes,Dariusz J. Gawryluk,Anna Krztoń-Maziopa,Vahid Sazgari,Yona Soh,Ming Shi,Milan Radović,Johan Chang,Steven Johnston,Nicholas C. Plumb*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文报道了在掺杂铋酸盐超导体 Ba₁₋ₓKₓBiO₃ 和 BaPb₁₋ᵧBiᵧO₃ 的费米面上观测到准粒子谱权重与散射率的周期性调制（类似铜氧化物中的"热点"模式），并证明其可由电子与沿 (π,π,π) 波矢、短程相干的涨落密度波耦合的模型描述，支持体系中存在由残余双极化子及其呼吸畸变构成的"二组分流"图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铋酸盐高温超导体被认为是潜在的双极化子系统，其中空穴对束缚于金属相中 BiO₆ 八面体的局域呼吸畸变，但获得这些准粒子及其激发的确凿证据一直十分困难，因此需要寻找直接实验证据来验证双极化子及其涨落的存在。

Method: 作者对掺杂铋酸盐 Ba₁₋ₓKₓBiO₃ 和 BaPb₁₋ᵧBiᵧO₃ 进行了电子结构（费米面）测量，分析准粒子谱权重和散射率的空间调制；随后构建理论模型，将观测特征解释为电子与沿 (π,π,π) 波矢、短程相干的涨落密度波调制相耦合，并与铜氧化物中的"热点"模式进行类比。

Result: 实验观测到两类掺杂铋酸盐的费米面上准粒子谱强度和散射率呈现周期性调制，类似铜氧化物超导体的"热点"花样；这些特征可由电子与短程相干的 (π,π,π) 密度波涨落耦合模型很好地描述，与残余双极化子及其 BiO₆ 呼吸畸变产生的涨落密度波相作用一致。

Conclusion: 该结果与体系中存在"二组分流"（未配对电子与由残余双极化子及 BiO₆ 八面体呼吸畸变构成的涨落密度波相互作用）的图像一致，为铋酸盐超导体中双极化子及其激发提供了证据。

Abstract: The bismuthate high-temperature superconductors have been identified as potential bipolaron systems, where hole pairs are bound to local breathing distortions of the BiO$_6$ octahedra in their metallic phase. However, obtaining definitive evidence for these quasiparticles and their excitations remains challenging. Here, we report on periodic modulations of the spectral intensities and scattering rates of quasiparticles on the Fermi surfaces of doped bismuthates Ba$_{1-x}$K$_x$BiO$_3$ and BaPb$_{1-y}$Bi$_y$O$_3$, which are reminiscent of ``hot spot'' patterns that have been observed in cuprate superconductors. We show that these features can be well described by a model where electrons couple to fluctuating density wave modulations with short-range coherence along a $(π,π,π)$-oriented wavevector. The results are consistent with the existence of a two-component fluid where unpaired electrons interact with a fluctuating density wave created by residual bipolarons and their breathing distortions of the BiO$_6$ octahedra.

</details>


### [106] [Crystal-Symmetry-Dependent Field-Free Superconducting Diode Effect in CoPt/Nb Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33717)
*He Yu,Jiaxin Chen,Yi Zhang,Gan Zhao,Yizhen Shao,Jiaxu Li,Yu He,Qiusha Wang,Yuanliang Zhu,Bin Han,Junda Qu,Haochang Lyu,Bowen Dong,Jie Qi,Dapeng Zhu,Daoqian Zhu,Yunchi Zhao,Liang Liu,Shouguo Wang,Weisheng Zhao,Guang Yang*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文在[111]取向的A1-CoPt/Nb外延异质结构中，实现了无需外磁场的超导二极管效应，并证明其临界电流不对称性由晶体学对称性（磁点群）决定，可用于超导磁态读出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应通常需要复杂器件结构和外磁场来同时破缺空间反演与时间反演对称性，严重限制了其在低温电子学和超导量子电路中的集成，因此亟需实现无外场的超导二极管效应并找到可工程化的调控自由度。

Method: 作者在[111]取向的A1-CoPt/Nb外延异质结构中制备超导二极管，通过改变面内电流方向（0°~60°旋转）和翻转CoPt磁化方向，系统测量临界电流不对称性；结合电流和温度依赖的二次谐波输运测量，分析无场电流诱导磁化翻转和涡旋介导的非互易输运机制；并以磁化依赖的二极管极性实现磁态的电子读出。

Result: 临界电流不对称性遵循磁点群对称关系：在低对称方向有限，沿磁镜面对称轴被抑制，并在60°面内旋转或CoPt磁化翻转时反转。相同的三重对称依赖也出现在无场电流诱导磁化翻转中，表明超导与正常态输运响应具有共同的晶体学起源。二次谐波测量结果与取向依赖界面边缘势垒引起的涡旋介导非互易性一致。

Conclusion: 该工作确立了晶体学对称性可作为工程化超导二极管效应的有效自由度，拓展了可用材料体系，并为无场超导电子学提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Superconducting diodes convert nonreciprocal critical currents into current-polarity-selective switching between zero-resistance and resistive states. However, realizing the superconducting diode effect often requires complex device architectures and an external magnetic field to break spatial-inversion and time-reversal symmetry, substantially limiting its integration into cryogenic electronics and superconducting quantum circuits. Here, we demonstrate precise control of the field-free superconducting diode effect in epitaxial [111]-oriented A1-CoPt/Nb heterostructures. The critical-current asymmetry obeys the magnetic-point-group symmetry relation: it is finite along low-symmetry directions, suppressed along magnetic mirror axes, and reverses under either a 60° in-plane rotation or reversal of the CoPt magnetization. The same threefold symmetry dependence is observed in field-free current-induced magnetization switching in A1-CoPt films, indicating a common crystallographic origin for the superconducting and normal-state transport responses. Current- and temperature-dependent second-harmonic transport measurements are consistent with vortex-mediated nonreciprocity arising from an orientation-dependent interfacial edge landscape. We further exploit the magnetization-dependent diode polarity for proof-of-concept superconducting readout of electrically written magnetic states. These results establish crystallographic symmetry as an effective degree of freedom for engineering the superconducting diode effect, expanding the accessible materials space and providing a practical route toward field-free superconducting electronics.

</details>


### [107] [Superconductivity from the Berry connection from many-electron wave functions: $U(1)$ gauge fluctuation, gauge invariance, and superconductivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33723)
*Hiroyasu Koizumi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文运用多电子波函数中的Berry联络及其涨落所导致的量子态间的概率性随机跃迁，重新审视电磁U(1)规范不变性与超导电性。经典电动力学中的规范不变性被视为提取不受规范涨落影响部分的要求；量子力学中存在不受规范涨落影响的耗散自由态，作者将其识别为超导态，并据此解释了磁场中可逆的超导-正常相变（含力的平衡）。该理论在保留BCS理论主要结果的同时，解决了当前标准理论中理论与实验之间的两个基本分歧。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前标准的超导理论（以BCS理论为基础）与实验之间存在两个基本分歧。作者试图从Berry联络及其涨落的视角重新理解电磁U(1)规范不变性，从而提出一套能够涵盖标准理论、同时消除既有理论与实验分歧的超导理论。

Method: 采用多电子波函数中的Berry联络，并通过分析Berry联络的涨落来描述量子态间的概率性随机跃迁。将经典电动力学中的规范不变性重新解释为"提取不受规范涨落影响部分"的约束条件，进而指出量子力学中存在不受规范涨落影响的耗散自由态，并将其认定为超导态。在此基础上对磁场中可逆的超导-正常相变及其力平衡给出解释。

Result: 该理论识别出与规范涨落无关的耗散自由态即为超导态，并成功解释了磁场中可逆的超导-正常相变（包括力的平衡机制）。同时，该理论在保留BCS理论主要结论的前提下，解决了当前标准理论中存在的两个理论与实验之间的基本分歧。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于Berry联络及其涨落的超导理论，不仅继承了BCS理论的主要成果，还消除了标准理论中两个长期存在的基本理论与实验分歧，因此可被视为一个涵盖当前标准理论的更广泛的超导理论。

Abstract: The electromagnetic $U(1)$ gauge invariance and superconductivity are examined by employing the Berry connection from many-electron wave functions and the fluctuations of it that give rise to the probabilistic random transitions among quantum states. The gauge invariance in the classical electrodynamics is regarded as the requirement that extracts unaffected parts by the gauge fluctuation. In quantum mechanics, dissipation-free states from the gauge fluctuation exist, and we identify them as superconducting states. We explain the reversible superconducting-normal phase transition in a magnetic field including the force balance. The present superconductivity theory lifts two fundamental disagreements between theory and experiment existing in the current standard theory with keeping the major results by the BCS theory. Thus, it may be considered as a superconductivity theory encompassing the current standard one.

</details>


### [108] [Superconductivity from a Cooper surface instability in a nodal Bose metal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33803)
*Jian-Jian Miao,Cui Ding*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 欠掺杂铜氧化物中，超导能隙最大处恰是低能电子谱权已在转变温度以上被耗尽之处；本文在动量空间 t–J 模型中证明，正常态是可精确求解的节点玻色金属，其沿"Cooper面"的配对磁化率对数发散，从而任意弱的配对转移吸引都能稳定节点 d 波超导体，并可用自旋分辨符合光电子能谱进行检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一个悖论式问题：在欠掺杂铜氧化物中，超导能隙最大的动量区域恰恰是单电子态密度在转变温度以上就已耗尽的区域，那么当单电子态密度趋于零时，微弱的吸引相互作用如何仍能驱动超导？这挑战了基于单电子态密度的传统 BCS 配对图像。

Method: 采用动量空间 t–J 模型，其正常态是可精确求解的"节点玻色金属"，具有四个单电子节点；在该正常态中存在两条称为 Cooper 面的轮廓，沿这些面添加或移除一对自旋单态、动量相反的电子不耗费能量，从而产生配对磁化率的对数发散，使任意弱的配对转移吸引即可稳定超导态，形成关联的节点 d 波超导体。

Result: 证明即使单电子态密度消失，Cooper 面上的对数发散配对磁化率仍允许任意微弱的配对转移吸引稳定关联节点 d 波超导；并预测在转变温度以上自旋分辨符合光电子能谱可分辨出两条零能对移除脊，在转变温度以下沿底层 Luttinger 面出现第三条脊。

Conclusion: 欠掺杂铜氧化物中的超导可由 Cooper 面配对机制而非传统单电子态密度机制驱动，弱吸引即可诱发节点 d 波超导；自旋分辨符合光电子能谱实验可通过观察转变温度上下的三条零能对移除/加入脊来检验该配对机制。

Abstract: In underdoped cuprates, the superconducting gap is largest where low-energy electron spectral weight is already depleted above the transition. How can a weak attraction drive superconductivity when the one-electron density of states vanishes? In a momentum-space \(t\)--\(J\) model, the exactly solvable normal state is a nodal Bose metal with four one-electron nodes. Along two contours called Cooper surfaces, adding or removing a spin-singlet pair of electrons with opposite momenta costs no energy. The resulting logarithmic divergence of the pair susceptibility allows an arbitrarily weak pair-transfer attraction to stabilize a correlated nodal \(d\)-wave superconductor. Spin-resolved coincidence photoemission could test this pairing mechanism by resolving two zero-energy pair-removal ridges above the transition and a third along the underlying Luttinger surface below it.

</details>


### [109] [Structural Anisotropy in Bi$_2$Se$_3$/$λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$ Topological Insulator/Superconductor Bilayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.33869)
*Fathan M. Santana,Arifa D. Agustin,Aulia A. Firdaus,Malik A. Baqiya,Darminto Darminto,Muhammad K. Nuryadin,Takahiko Sasaki,Isao Watanabe,Takuya Kobayashi,Hiromi Taniguchi,Yasuyuki Ishii,Pasquale Marra,Paolo Mele,Dita P. Sari*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 本文研究了 λ-(BETS)₂GaCl₄（λ-Ga）有机超导体对 Bi₂Se₃ 沉积响应的晶向依赖性，发现其结构与计算电子响应存在显著各向异性，其中 ab 面在约 110 nm（沉积 4 分钟）的中间沉积阶段响应最大，而 ac 面几乎不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究有机超导体 λ-Ga 与拓扑绝缘体 Bi₂Se₃ 异质结构中，晶体取向如何影响沉积诱导的结构与电子性质变化，以理解界面各向异性行为。

Method: 采用截面聚焦离子束（FIB）成像确认 Bi₂Se₃ 沉积层与 λ-Ga 的界面形成；利用单晶 X 射线衍射（SC-XRD）解析镀膜后晶体的结构变化；基于精修结构进行第一性原理计算评估电子性质修饰；并结合 SiO₂ 衬底上 Bi₂Se₃ 薄膜的光学光谱研究沉积时间依赖的膜层响应。

Result: FIB 证实形成了含 Bi₂Se₃ 的沉积层及清晰界面；SC-XRD 显示镀膜后 λ-Ga 晶体结构保持完整，但响应强烈依赖晶向且呈非单调变化——沉积 4 分钟时 ab 面修饰最大，ac 面几乎不变，该条件对应约 110 nm 的中间沉积阶段；第一性原理计算在相同条件下呈现最大的取向依赖电子修饰；光学光谱显示 Bi₂Se₃ 膜响应随沉积时间演化。

Conclusion: λ-Ga 对 Bi₂Se₃ 沉积的响应存在显著的结构与电子各向异性，且在中间沉积阶段（约 110 nm）最为突出，为有机超导体-拓扑绝缘体异质界面的取向调控提供了依据。

Abstract: We investigate the crystallographic-orientation dependence of the response of $λ$-(BETS)$_2$GaCl$_4$ ($λ$-Ga) to Bi$_2$Se$_3$ deposition. Cross-sectional focused-ion-beam (FIB) imaging confirms formation of a deposited Bi$_2$Se$_3$-containing layer and a distinct interface with $λ$-Ga. Single-crystal X-ray diffraction (SC-XRD) shows that the coated $λ$-Ga crystal remains structurally intact but exhibits a strongly orientation-dependent, non-monotonic response, with the largest modification for the $ab$-plane after 4 min of deposition, whereas the $ac$-plane remains comparatively unchanged. This condition corresponds to the intermediate-deposition regime previously identified at a Bi$_2$Se$_3$ thickness of approximately 110 nm. First-principles calculations based on the refined structures show the largest orientation-dependent electronic modification under the same conditions. Complementary optical spectroscopy of Bi$_2$Se$_3$/SiO$_2$ films shows deposition-time-dependent evolution of the film response. These results establish a pronounced crystallographic anisotropy in the structural and calculated electronic response of $λ$-Ga to Bi$_2$Se$_3$ deposition.

</details>
